<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50378228" data-id="50378228" data-tags="Security" data-score="2978" data-comments="747" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228" data-time="1793881142">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sshhole.com/receipts/3bsd-passwd.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sshhole.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>sshole</span><span class='zh'>sshole</span></a><span class="dom">sshhole.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016. The issue relates to how Unix historically represented accounts that should never permit login, including accounts marked with an asterisk in the password file. The behavior can therefore expose accounts that were intended to be inaccessible.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。问题与 Unix 处理本应禁止登录的账户的历史方式有关，包括在密码文件中用星号标记的账户。因此，原本应不可访问的账户可能被暴露。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>2978</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>747</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50341174" data-id="50341174" data-tags="AI" data-score="2928" data-comments="880" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50341174" data-time="1793643688">
<a class="thumb" href="https://rikiyat.github.io/blog/weight-space-programming/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://rikiyat.github.io/images/blog/weight-space-programming.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='rikiyat.github.io' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rikiyat.github.io/blog/weight-space-programming/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astra can read and write weights to improve Qwen</span><span class='zh'>Astra 可读写权重以改进 Qwen</span></a><span class="dom">rikiyat.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The source reports that frontier LLMs can read and write neural-network weights directly.</span><span class='zh'>资料称，前沿大语言模型已经能够直接读取和写入神经网络权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source reports that frontier LLMs can read and write neural-network weights directly. GPT-6 Astra can infer what a network was trained to do from its raw weights and create small game-playing networks that outperform ones trained with PPO for 100 million steps. The capability could matter for model optimization and interpretability.</span><span class='zh'>资料称，前沿大语言模型已经能够直接读取和写入神经网络权重。GPT-6 Astra 可以仅凭原始权重判断网络的训练目标，还能从零编写击败经过 1 亿步 PPO 训练的小型游戏网络。这种能力可能对模型优化和可解释性具有重要意义。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>2928</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50341174' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>880</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50319510" data-id="50319510" data-tags="Web" data-score="2881" data-comments="637" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319510" data-time="1793476613">
<a class="thumb" href="https://karpathy.bearblog.dev/hacker-tube/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=karpathy.bearblog.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>karpathy.bearblog.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://karpathy.bearblog.dev/hacker-tube/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HackerTube: YouTube built from videos recommended by HN</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube：由 HN 推荐视频构成的 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">karpathy.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007. The collection is available through hackertube.org. It turns years of community recommendations into a browsable video archive.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。该视频集合可通过 hackertube.org 浏览。它将多年社区推荐整理成了一个可探索的视频档案。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>2881</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319510' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>637</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50326821" data-id="50326821" data-tags="Show HN|Data" data-score="1843" data-comments="304" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50326821" data-time="1793548708">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hackerbooks.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=hackerbooks.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>hackerbooks.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hackerbooks.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: HackerBooks – HackerTube, but for books mentioned on HN</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：HackerBooks——面向 HN 提及书籍的 HackerTube</span></a><span class="dom">hackerbooks.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerBooks catalogs about 16,500 books mentioned by at least two different Hacker News commenters between 2007 and today.</span><span class='zh'>HackerBooks 收录了 2007 年至今至少被两名不同 Hacker News 评论者提到过的约 1.65 万本书。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerBooks catalogs about 16,500 books mentioned by at least two different Hacker News commenters between 2007 and today. Each book links back to the comments that mentioned it, and the dataset can be downloaded from the about page. The creator notes that the system counts mentions without distinguishing praise from criticism, so some entries may be missing or mismatched.</span><span class='zh'>HackerBooks 收录了 2007 年至今至少被两名不同 Hacker News 评论者提到过的约 1.65 万本书。每本书都链接回提及它的评论，数据集也可从关于页面下载。创建者说明，系统只统计提及次数，不区分赞扬和批评，因此部分书籍可能遗漏或匹配错误。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1843</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50326821' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>304</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50337608" data-id="50337608" data-tags="Security|AI" data-score="1609" data-comments="671" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50337608" data-time="1793631120">
<a class="thumb" href="https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=chalmovsky.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>chalmovsky.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WTF Ubuntu?</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu，到底怎么回事？</span></a><span class="dom">chalmovsky.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is described as a critical, remotely exploitable bug in the Linux kernel&#x27;s TCP code that has existed since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 被描述为 Linux 内核 TCP 代码中的严重漏洞，自 2016 年起便存在且可远程利用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is described as a critical, remotely exploitable bug in the Linux kernel&#x27;s TCP code that has existed since 2016. The source says Anthropic developed an exploit capable of gaining root access, while Debian issued a patch on October 16 and supported Ubuntu releases remained listed as vulnerable.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 被描述为 Linux 内核 TCP 代码中的严重漏洞，自 2016 年起便存在且可远程利用。资料称 Anthropic 已开发出可获取服务器 root 权限的漏洞利用程序；Debian 于 10 月 16 日发布了补丁，而受支持的 Ubuntu 版本仍被列为易受攻击。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1609</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50337608' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>671</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50385272" data-id="50385272" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Security|Code" data-score="1605" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272" data-time="1793917035">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends your email address to websites</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将你的邮箱地址发送给网站</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches. The user says this happened without permission and was followed by a large increase in scam emails. They argue that the behavior should be removed rather than controlled by an opt-in or opt-out setting.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。该用户表示，未经允许发生这一行为后，自己收到的诈骗邮件大幅增加。用户认为，这种行为不应通过加入或退出选项控制，而应从系统中彻底移除。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1605</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>533</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50364336" data-id="50364336" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="1271" data-comments="743" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336" data-time="1793793137">
<a class="thumb" href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=68544156&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C312%2C0%2C313' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='spectrum.ieee.org' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is forcing us to pay off 40 years of technical debt</span><span class='zh'>AI 正迫使我们偿还 40 年积累的技术债</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity. That acceleration is exposing painful technical debt in fragile legacy systems built up over roughly four decades. The pressure from AI is therefore forcing organizations to address weaknesses that older systems had left unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。这样的加速暴露了脆弱遗留系统中积累约 40 年的严重技术债。AI 带来的压力因此迫使组织处理旧系统长期遗留下来的弱点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1271</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>743</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50371246" data-id="50371246" data-tags="Security|Privacy|Politics" data-score="1249" data-comments="431" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246" data-time="1793825118">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">08</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Residential proxies detected on White House network</span><span class='zh'>白宫网络被发现使用住宅代理</span></a><span class="dom">layer3intel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services. The network, AS2650, is registered to the Executive Office of the President, which hosts the White House network. Residential proxies route customers’ traffic through everyday devices so it appears to originate from those devices’ networks.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。该网络的 AS2650 注册于负责托管白宫网络的美国总统行政办公室。住宅代理会让客户的流量经由普通设备转发，使其看起来来自这些设备所在的网络。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1249</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>431</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50391456" data-id="50391456" data-tags="Business" data-score="1135" data-comments="2046" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50391456" data-time="1793967231">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2025-02/250213-Cybertruck-aa-1045-dd55f3.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nbcnews.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">09</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tesla to wind down Cybertruck production</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉将逐步停止生产 Cybertruck</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027. He said the move will make room for higher production of the newly launched Cybercab, central to Tesla&#x27;s robotaxi plans. Cybertruck sales have remained weak, and it has sold in only a limited number of markets. It remains unclear whether production will move elsewhere or end entirely.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。他称，此举将为提高新款 Cybercab 的产量腾出空间，而 Cybercab 是特斯拉机器人出租车计划的核心车型。Cybertruck 销量长期疲软，目前仅在少数市场销售。该车型是转移到其他工厂生产还是彻底停产，目前尚不清楚。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/libghostty-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libghostty 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Libghostty 1.0</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core. Both the library and its C ABI are now stable. The C API has grown from nonexistent to a complete terminal emulator used in production by millions of people across hundreds of free and commercial products.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。该库本身及其 C ABI 现已稳定。其 C API 已从无到有发展为完整的终端模拟器，目前已在数百款免费和商业产品中投入生产，服务数百万人。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1077</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50399933' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>194</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50351111" data-id="50351111" data-tags="AI" data-score="1030" data-comments="207" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50351111" data-time="1793708477">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://honnibal.dev/blog/astra-read-write-misleading" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The &#x27;Astra can read and write weights to improve Qwen&#x27; post is misleading</span><span class='zh'>“Astra 能读写权重来改进 Qwen”的说法具有误导性</span></a><span class="dom">honnibal.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that the experiments reading raw model weights and the experiments modifying Qwen use entirely different techniques.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，读取模型原始权重的实验与修改 Qwen 的实验使用了完全不同的技术。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that the experiments reading raw model weights and the experiments modifying Qwen use entirely different techniques. They compare the presentation to a series of stage-magic tricks that appear to test competing explanations while relying on different mechanisms.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，读取模型原始权重的实验与修改 Qwen 的实验使用了完全不同的技术。作者将这种呈现方式比作一连串舞台魔术：表面上是在检验相互竞争的解释，实际却依赖不同机制。文章分析了原始说法为何可能让人误解这些实验究竟证明了什么。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.nns.ee/2026/11/02/renault-hack/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jailbreaking my car&#x27;s navigation system just so I can change the time</span><span class='zh'>为了改时间，我破解了汽车的导航系统</span></a><span class="dom">blog.nns.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes modifying the navigation system of a 2014 Renault Latitude to change the dashboard clock.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了如何修改一辆 2014 年雷诺 Latitude 的导航系统，以调整仪表盘时钟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes modifying the navigation system of a 2014 Renault Latitude to change the dashboard clock. The car apparently required a navigation-map SD card for access to the time-setting function, even though the card had been discarded because its maps were outdated. Without it, the display showed a prominent “No maps found!” message.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了如何修改一辆 2014 年雷诺 Latitude 的导航系统，以调整仪表盘时钟。该车似乎需要导航地图 SD 卡才能访问时间设置功能，但由于地图过时，这张卡此前已被丢弃。没有 SD 卡时，显示屏会显示醒目的“No maps found!”提示。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/italian-farmer-found-unknown-seeds-attic-with-nothing-lose-planted-them-result-biblical-180989644/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/_-X8sWyx82CD0eX5ZxIF7dcS2X8=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1181x787:1182x788)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/37/95/3795362b-64a6-4d5f-b7df-67aa18192521/l2190910.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='smithsonianmag.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/italian-farmer-found-unknown-seeds-attic-with-nothing-lose-planted-them-result-biblical-180989644/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An Italian farmer found some unknown seeds in his attic and planted them</span><span class='zh'>意大利农民在阁楼发现不明种子并将其种下</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915. After he discarded the seeds in manure, several types of wheat unexpectedly sprouted. One variety grew about six feet tall and produced seven ears, unlike the single-ear commercial wheat Grossi knew.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。他把种子倒进肥料堆后，几种小麦竟意外发芽。其中一个品种长到约六英尺高，并结出七个麦穗，不同于格罗西熟悉的单穗商业小麦。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>870</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320963' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>236</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50403097" data-id="50403097" data-tags="Privacy" data-score="850" data-comments="450" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50403097" data-time="1794044763">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://8rank.ai/game/apple-intelligence-personal-knowledge-graph" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every Mac comes with an (undocumented) family member classifier</span><span class='zh'>每台 Mac 都自带一个未公开的家庭成员分类器</span></a><span class="dom">8rank.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities. The graph is described as covering contacts, relationships, communications, locations, activities, and timing.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。该图谱据称涵盖联系人、彼此关系、通信、位置、活动及时间信息。文章还分析了这一系统的工作方式，并提供了查看个人设备上该图谱的工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://rhaas.blogspot.com/2026/10/postgres-19-is-late-and-its-ais-fault.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres 19 is late because so many more bugs are being found now</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 19 因发现的错误大幅增加而延期</span></a><span class="dom">rhaas.blogspot.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Postgres 19 is late because many more bugs are being found during development.</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 19 的发布出现延期，原因是开发过程中发现的错误数量大幅增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Postgres 19 is late because many more bugs are being found during development. The title identifies the delay and attributes it to the increased discovery of defects.</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 19 的发布出现延期，原因是开发过程中发现的错误数量大幅增加。标题将这次延迟归因于缺陷发现增多。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision. During prerelease testing, Anthropic says the models helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, make progress on robotics evaluations, and solve problems in physics. The company also says the models formalized a proof of Fermat’s Last Theorem in 30 hours.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。在预发布测试中，Anthropic 表示这些模型帮助工程师完成软件迁移、设计出三种可运行的电子设备、推进机器人评测，并解决物理学问题。公司还称，模型在 30 小时内形式化完成了费马大定理的证明。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stringman is presented as an $800 open-source room-scale gripper robot.</span><span class='zh'>Stringman 被介绍为一款售价 800 美元的开源房间级抓取机器人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stringman is presented as an $800 open-source room-scale gripper robot. The available material provides an installation note about securing cleats to wall studs or columns, but no further technical specifications.</span><span class='zh'>Stringman 被介绍为一款售价 800 美元的开源房间级抓取机器人。现有内容只提到应将固定件用木螺钉固定在墙柱上，或用棘轮绑带固定在立柱上，未提供更多技术规格。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340088" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Subvocal – Talk to your computer without making noise</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Subvocal——无需发声即可与电脑交谈</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Subvocal is building smart glasses that decode silently mouthed words into text at speech speed.</span><span class='zh'>Subvocal 正在开发能将无声口型以接近语速转换为文字的智能眼镜。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Subvocal is building smart glasses that decode silently mouthed words into text at speech speed. The system can also work without opening the mouth, using radio-frequency sensing to detect movements inside the mouth, though the developers distinguish this from silently thinking words. The founders began the project after finding that voice tools were impractical in libraries, offices, and public spaces.</span><span class='zh'>Subvocal 正在开发能将无声口型以接近语速转换为文字的智能眼镜。系统甚至可以在不张嘴的情况下工作，通过射频感知口腔内部动作；开发者强调，这不同于单纯在脑中默念。创始人因发现语音工具不适合图书馆、办公室和公共场所，开始了这个项目。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>817</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340088' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>388</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50424581" data-id="50424581" data-tags="Education|Data" data-score="799" data-comments="133" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50424581" data-time="1794226762">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.foldableflight.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fold Epic Paper Airplanes</span><span class='zh'>折叠史诗级纸飞机</span></a><span class="dom">foldableflight.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Foldable Flight presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials.</span><span class='zh'>Foldable Flight 提供了一个大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种纸飞机配备分步骤视频折叠教程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Foldable Flight presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials. Its listed prototypes include one that can fly more than 300 feet and another, named Unite, that holds a 31.2-second flight-time record.</span><span class='zh'>Foldable Flight 提供了一个大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种纸飞机配备分步骤视频折叠教程。其展示的原型中，有一种飞行距离超过 300 英尺，另一种名为 Unite 的纸飞机创下了 31.2 秒的滞空时间纪录。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.bosscher.ch/posts/openstick/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What I run on my $10 USD OpenStick homelab</span><span class='zh'>我在 10 美元 OpenStick 家庭实验室上运行的服务</span></a><span class="dom">blog.bosscher.ch</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author bought an OpenStick for $10 including shipping, mainly because it was an unusual and appealing piece of hardware. Although the device is less powerful than many modern watches, it can still support enjoyable self-hosting experiments. The project demonstrates how a very inexpensive, tiny computer can serve as an entry point to homelabbing.</span><span class='zh'>作者花 10 美元（含运费）买了一台 OpenStick，主要是因为这款硬件新奇而有趣。它的性能可能还不如许多现代智能手表，但仍然适合进行有趣的自托管实验。这个项目展示了廉价小设备如何成为入门家庭实验室的起点。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf/opengraph-image' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='atlasum.net' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TCP Use-After-Free: CVE-2026-98432 / CVSS 9.8</span><span class='zh'>TCP 释放后使用：CVE-2026-98432，CVSS 9.8</span></a><span class="dom">atlasum.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer. Stable kernels fixed it in October 2026, with patches for versions including 5.10.272 through 7.3, but the flaw dates back to Linux 4.4. At the reported time, Ubuntu still marked every release as vulnerable, while Red Hat listed RHEL 8, 9, and 10 as affected without a fix.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。稳定版内核已于 2026 年 10 月修复，补丁覆盖 5.10.272 至 7.3 等版本，但该缺陷可追溯至 Linux 4.4。报道所述时间点，Ubuntu 仍将所有版本标为易受攻击，Red Hat 也列出受影响的 RHEL 8、9 和 10，尚未提供修复。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>747</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319143' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>333</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50410107" data-id="50410107" data-tags="AI" data-score="732" data-comments="451" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410107" data-time="1794107508">
<a class="thumb" href="https://caleb.software/posts/claude-to-pi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://caleb.software/assets/img/claude-to-pi/vsc-disconnected.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='caleb.software' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://caleb.software/posts/claude-to-pi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye Claude, hello Pi</span><span class='zh'>告别 Claude，迎接 Pi</span></a><span class="dom">caleb.software</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription. Despite considering its release an important moment for software development, the author accumulated enough small frustrations to stop using it as the main coding harness. Their development setup is now being migrated to Pi.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。尽管作者认为它的发布可能会成为软件开发史上的重要节点，但长期积累的许多小问题最终让作者不再把它作为主要编程工具。作者目前正将开发环境迁移到 Pi。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ppmpreetham/BUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender&#x27;s UI and Runtime: Much Faster Than GPUI</span><span class='zh'>Blender 的 UI 与运行时：比 GPUI 快得多</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase. It supports Rust, Python, JavaScript, React, C, and C++. The project describes its performance as twice that of GPUI.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。它支持 Rust、Python、JavaScript、React、C 和 C++。项目方称其性能是 GPUI 的两倍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50328896" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Which TV do you recommend for privacy?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：你推荐哪款重视隐私的电视？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user is looking for a privacy-respecting television with a 60–80-inch 4K display running at least 60 Hz.</span><span class='zh'>一名用户想寻找重视隐私的电视，要求尺寸在 60 至 80 英寸之间、支持 4K 且刷新率至少为 60Hz。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user is looking for a privacy-respecting television with a 60–80-inch 4K display running at least 60 Hz. They want the device to act only as a display for an Apple TV rather than use smart-TV apps, and are asking which models pair well with that setup.</span><span class='zh'>一名用户想寻找重视隐私的电视，要求尺寸在 60 至 80 英寸之间、支持 4K 且刷新率至少为 60Hz。他希望电视只作为 Apple TV 的显示器，而不运行智能电视原生应用，并询问哪些型号适合这种配置。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>726</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50328896' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>871</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50355251" data-id="50355251" data-tags="AI" data-score="725" data-comments="212" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50355251" data-time="1793727600">
<a class="thumb" href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Nov/3/qwen-can-interpret-model-weights/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=simonwillison.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>simonwillison.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">27</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Nov/3/qwen-can-interpret-model-weights/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8 27B on my laptop can interpret model weights, too</span><span class='zh'>我笔记本上的 Qwen 3.8 27B 也能解读模型权重</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author reports that Qwen 3.8 27B running on a laptop can interpret raw neural-network weights.</span><span class='zh'>作者称，在笔记本上运行的 Qwen 3.8 27B 也能解读神经网络的原始权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author reports that Qwen 3.8 27B running on a laptop can interpret raw neural-network weights. This follows research describing “weight-space programming,” in which language models analyze and modify model weights represented as floating-point numbers. The earlier experiments highlighted frontier-model capabilities, including those attributed to GPT-6 Astra.</span><span class='zh'>作者称，在笔记本上运行的 Qwen 3.8 27B 也能解读神经网络的原始权重。这延续了“权重空间编程”的研究：语言模型可以分析并修改由浮点数表示的模型权重。此前的实验展示了前沿模型的这类能力，包括研究归于 GPT-6 Astra 的能力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>725</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50355251' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>212</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50363461" data-id="50363461" data-tags="Privacy|AI" data-score="715" data-comments="189" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" data-time="1793787027">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">28</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Gemini trains on chat history, even for subscribers (personal accounts)</span><span class='zh'>告诉 HN：Gemini 会用聊天记录训练，即使是订阅用户的个人账户也不例外</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>For personal Google AI subscriptions, saved Gemini chats can be used to improve Google services and train generative AI models when Keep Activity is enabled.</span><span class='zh'>对于个人 Google AI 订阅账户，在启用“保留活动记录”时，保存的 Gemini 聊天记录可用于改进 Google 服务和训练生成式 AI 模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>For personal Google AI subscriptions, saved Gemini chats can be used to improve Google services and train generative AI models when Keep Activity is enabled. A subset of chats may be reviewed by human reviewers and can be retained for up to three years after review, even if the user deletes activity. Users are warned not to enter confidential information, and opting out requires disabling chat history or using temporary chats.</span><span class='zh'>对于个人 Google AI 订阅账户，在启用“保留活动记录”时，保存的 Gemini 聊天记录可用于改进 Google 服务和训练生成式 AI 模型。部分聊天可能由人工审核，审核过的内容即使用户删除活动记录，也可能保留最长三年。用户被提醒不要输入机密信息；若要退出，需要关闭聊天记录或使用临时聊天。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>715</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>189</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50422069" data-id="50422069" data-tags="AI" data-score="708" data-comments="459" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069" data-time="1794207865">
<a class="thumb" href="https://twitter.com/shanemelaugh/status/2119192075505893695" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HWjhsqYWMAALhKb?format=webp&amp;name=large' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='twitter.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/shanemelaugh/status/2119192075505893695" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is Opus 5.5 nerfed? Thinking patterns changed on November 3</span><span class='zh'>Opus 5.5 被削弱了吗？11 月 3 日推理模式发生变化</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3. The model name did not change, but the analysis claims that its thinking patterns shifted noticeably. The report does not identify the cause of the change.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。模型名称并未改变，但分析认为其思考模式出现了显著转折。该报告没有说明变化原因。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>708</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>459</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50415628" data-id="50415628" data-tags="AI|Programming|Finance" data-score="707" data-comments="391" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628" data-time="1794161767">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.thestateofai.com/media/uploads/33f913ce812c4074d207c01b6df1858a692e6bc3229dadbfcd4f56b61722a538.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thestateofai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">30</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs is pulling Claude Code from its bankers and traders</span><span class='zh'>高盛将从银行家和交易员手中收回 Claude Code</span></a><span class="dom">thestateofai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers. Those employees had used the tools to track client flows and build market models, while the bank had already barred Hong Kong bankers from Claude in April. The guidance followed a review of productivity and risk concerns and came weeks before Goldman was due to act as a lead underwriter for Anthropic&#x27;s IPO.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。此前，这些员工曾用相关工具追踪客户资金流向并构建市场模型；高盛也已于 4 月禁止香港银行家使用 Claude。这项指导是在审查生产力和风险问题后出台的，时间上距离高盛担任 Anthropic IPO 主承销商仅数周。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>707</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>391</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50384233" data-id="50384233" data-tags="Security" data-score="704" data-comments="276" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50384233" data-time="1793911422">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2026-83844" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=cve.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>cve.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">31</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2026-83844" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MS Paint Remote Code Execution Vulnerability</span><span class='zh'>MS Paint 远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">cve.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A remote code execution vulnerability has been reported in Microsoft Paint.</span><span class='zh'>微软画图工具被报告存在远程代码执行漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A remote code execution vulnerability has been reported in Microsoft Paint. The issue could allow code to run remotely when exploited.</span><span class='zh'>微软画图工具被报告存在远程代码执行漏洞。攻击者利用该问题后，可能在远程执行代码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>704</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50384233' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>276</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50336518" data-id="50336518" data-tags="Web" data-score="654" data-comments="152" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50336518" data-time="1793625935">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.mistys-internet.website/post/2026/10/29/if-you-love-a-website-back-it-up/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you love a website, back it up</span><span class='zh'>如果你喜欢一个网站，就把它备份下来</span></a><span class="dom">blog.mistys-internet.website</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes discovering that the long-running Teapuppies fansite had disappeared and its domain was serving online-gambling advertisements.</span><span class='zh'>作者发现，一个长期存在的 Teapuppies 粉丝网站已经消失，原域名如今被用于投放在线赌博广告。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes discovering that the long-running Teapuppies fansite had disappeared and its domain was serving online-gambling advertisements. The site had been online since 2004 and largely unchanged since 2005, making its loss an example of how fragile old web pages can be.</span><span class='zh'>作者发现，一个长期存在的 Teapuppies 粉丝网站已经消失，原域名如今被用于投放在线赌博广告。该网站自 2004 年上线、2005 年后基本未变，这次消失体现了旧网页保存状况的脆弱性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kagi.com/changelog#11820" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Search for everyone</span><span class='zh'>面向所有人的搜索</span></a><span class="dom">kagi.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kagi has opened web search to anyone without requiring an account for the first 50 searches.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 现在允许任何人使用网页搜索，前 50 次搜索无需创建账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kagi has opened web search to anyone without requiring an account for the first 50 searches. Registering adds another 100 searches. The paid search engine says it does not run ads and is relying on community recommendations and word of mouth.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 现在允许任何人使用网页搜索，前 50 次搜索无需创建账户。注册后还可额外获得 100 次搜索。这个付费搜索引擎表示自己不投放广告，而是依靠社区推荐和口碑传播。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PrivSec-dev/banking-apps-compat-report/issues/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dutch &#x27;idealist&#x27; bank ASN blocks non-Google Android, including GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>荷兰“理想主义”银行 ASN 屏蔽非 Google Android，包括 GrapheneOS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS. The report identifies app version 2.40.2 in the Netherlands. One user reported that the app appeared to work on a stock operating system.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。报告涉及荷兰地区的 2.40.2 版本。一名用户表示，该应用在原生操作系统上看起来可以正常运行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>648</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50390124' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>424</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50366999" data-id="50366999" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="627" data-comments="186" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999" data-time="1793806230">
<a class="thumb" href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='huggingface.co' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3.1 Pro model weights are out</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3.1 Pro 模型权重已发布</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window. It was post-trained from Kimi K3 using large-scale joint reinforcement learning across hundreds of domains. The model replaces Gated MLA with Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention for more efficient long-context modeling.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。这是一款开放权重、原生多模态的智能体模型，拥有 2.8 万亿参数和 100 万 token 的上下文窗口。该模型基于 Kimi K3，并通过覆盖数百个领域的大规模联合强化学习进行后训练；同时以 Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention 替代 Gated MLA，以提高长上下文建模效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>627</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>186</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50379704" data-id="50379704" data-tags="Security" data-score="621" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379704" data-time="1793889860">
<a class="thumb" href="https://alpinelinux.org/posts/2026-11-05-openssh-password-authentication-bypass/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=alpinelinux.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>alpinelinux.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">36</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alpinelinux.org/posts/2026-11-05-openssh-password-authentication-bypass/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alpine Linux Security Advisory: OpenSSH-server password authentication bypass</span><span class='zh'>Alpine Linux 安全公告：OpenSSH-server 绕过密码认证</span></a><span class="dom">alpinelinux.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alpine Linux advises users running sshd to update openssh immediately and restart the daemon.</span><span class='zh'>Alpine Linux 建议运行 sshd 的用户立即更新 openssh 并重启守护进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alpine Linux advises users running sshd to update openssh immediately and restart the daemon. Updating the package alone does not replace an already running sshd process. Until updating is possible, disabling password logins with PasswordAuthentication no and restarting sshd closes the hole.</span><span class='zh'>Alpine Linux 建议运行 sshd 的用户立即更新 openssh 并重启守护进程。仅更新软件包并不会替换已经运行的 sshd 进程。在完成更新前，可将配置设为 PasswordAuthentication no 并重启 sshd，以关闭该漏洞。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>621</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379704' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50362905" data-id="50362905" data-tags="Hardware" data-score="612" data-comments="287" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50362905" data-time="1793781989">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.mindstudio.ai/blog/linux-nvidia-rtx-spark-laptop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.mscdn.ai/o/iZl0kkZU2R9KXyy7/a/3V2ICO3oSq2QZPii/_published/generated-images/0d8c9668-cbfe-4ecf-bb31-1a7fff7102be.png?fm=auto&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=crop' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='mindstudio.ai' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">37</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mindstudio.ai/blog/linux-nvidia-rtx-spark-laptop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia RTX Spark encrypted firmware slows inference speed to 35% on Linux</span><span class='zh'>英伟达 RTX Spark 在 Linux 上因加密固件导致推理速度降至 35%</span></a><span class="dom">mindstudio.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RTX Spark laptops run Linux with GPU acceleration through Nvidia’s open kernel driver, but their GPU clocks drop to roughly 780 MHz when plugged in, instead of exceeding 2 GHz on Windows.</span><span class='zh'>RTX Spark 笔记本可以在 Linux 上运行，并通过英伟达开源内核驱动实现 GPU 加速，但接通电源后 GPU 频率会被限制在约 780MHz，而 Windows 上可超过 2GHz。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RTX Spark laptops run Linux with GPU acceleration through Nvidia’s open kernel driver, but their GPU clocks drop to roughly 780 MHz when plugged in, instead of exceeding 2 GHz on Windows. On a 120-billion-parameter model, performance falls from about 65 tokens per second to fewer than 14. The problem is tied to power-management firmware that Linux currently cannot read.</span><span class='zh'>RTX Spark 笔记本可以在 Linux 上运行，并通过英伟达开源内核驱动实现 GPU 加速，但接通电源后 GPU 频率会被限制在约 780MHz，而 Windows 上可超过 2GHz。在一个 1200 亿参数模型上，速度因此从约每秒 65 个 token 降至不足 14 个。问题与 Linux 目前无法读取的电源管理固件有关。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>612</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50362905' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>287</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50341462" data-id="50341462" data-tags="Security" data-score="610" data-comments="205" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50341462" data-time="1793644778">
<a class="thumb" href="https://freenode.net/article/ubuntu-lts-kernels-still-unfixed-for-critical-cve-2026-98432" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=freenode.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>freenode.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">38</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://freenode.net/article/ubuntu-lts-kernels-still-unfixed-for-critical-cve-2026-98432" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ubuntu still has not released patch for Linux 0day RCE in almost 3 weeks</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu 近三周仍未为 Linux 0day RCE 发布补丁</span></a><span class="dom">freenode.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ubuntu has not released patched kernels for CVE-2026-98432, a vulnerability rated CVSS 9.8.</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu 尚未为 CVSS 评分 9.8 的 CVE-2026-98432 发布修复内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ubuntu has not released patched kernels for CVE-2026-98432, a vulnerability rated CVSS 9.8. Ubuntu 22.04, 24.04, and 26.04 LTS therefore lack an official kernel package that fixes the issue. Internet-facing and multi-tenant systems relying on those kernels remain particularly exposed until updates become available.</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu 尚未为 CVSS 评分 9.8 的 CVE-2026-98432 发布修复内核。Ubuntu 22.04、24.04 和 26.04 LTS 因此仍没有能够修复该问题的官方内核包。在更新发布前，依赖这些内核的面向互联网和多租户系统尤其处于暴露状态。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>610</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50341462' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>205</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50365755" data-id="50365755" data-tags="Security|AI|Finance" data-score="601" data-comments="369" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50365755" data-time="1793801395">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/11/03/north-korean-fake-job-interview-led-to-63b-binance-heist/5307398" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://image.theregister.com/254036.jpg?imageId=254036&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='theregister.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">39</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/security/2026/11/03/north-korean-fake-job-interview-led-to-63b-binance-heist/5307398" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>North Korean fake job interview led to $6.3B Binance heist</span><span class='zh'>朝鲜假面试引发 63 亿美元币安劫案</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>According to Binance, North Korean cryptocurrency thieves tricked a software engineer into downloading malware during a fake job interview.</span><span class='zh'>币安称，朝鲜加密货币窃贼在一次虚假求职面试中诱骗一名软件工程师下载恶意软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>According to Binance, North Korean cryptocurrency thieves tricked a software engineer into downloading malware during a fake job interview. The exchange said the October 29 breach involved social engineering, malware, compromised internal AI tooling, endpoint takeover, and an advanced transaction-authorization method. Binance characterized it as a combination of techniques rather than a single isolated phishing incident.</span><span class='zh'>币安称，朝鲜加密货币窃贼在一次虚假求职面试中诱骗一名软件工程师下载恶意软件。该交易所表示，10 月 29 日的入侵涉及社会工程、恶意软件、遭入侵的内部 AI 工具、终端接管以及一种先进的交易授权方法。币安将其描述为多种手段共同造成的事件，而非单一的钓鱼攻击。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code&#x27;s &quot;Don&#x27;t Ask Again&quot; feature exposed passwords in 1,122 public GitHub files.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的“不要再询问”功能导致密码出现在 1,122 个公开 GitHub 文件中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code&#x27;s &quot;Don&#x27;t Ask Again&quot; feature exposed passwords in 1,122 public GitHub files.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 的“不要再询问”功能导致密码出现在 1,122 个公开 GitHub 文件中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/cpus/amd-expects-to-outsell-intel-in-q4-for-the-first-time-in-history-usd17b-guidance-would-exceed-intels-projections" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AMD expects to outsell Intel in Q4 for the first time in history</span><span class='zh'>AMD 预计第四季度历史首次超过英特尔</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD expects to outsell Intel in the fourth quarter for the first time in its history.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 预计将在第四季度历史上首次超过英特尔。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AMD expects to outsell Intel in the fourth quarter for the first time in its history. Record quarterly revenue is being driven by the AI boom, while its client and gaming businesses are facing double-digit declines heading into 2027. The forecast indicates a widening contrast between AMD’s AI growth and its weaker traditional segments.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 预计将在第四季度历史上首次超过英特尔。AI 热潮推动其季度营收创下纪录，但面向客户的业务和游戏业务在进入 2027 年时正面临两位数下滑。这一预测显示，AMD 的 AI 业务增长与传统业务疲软之间的差距正在扩大。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Gaming' style='--c:#a855f7'>Gaming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>578</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50367125' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>221</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50389222" data-id="50389222" data-tags="Politics" data-score="577" data-comments="437" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50389222" data-time="1793949724">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/business/world-at-work/britain-make-salary-disclosure-job-ads-mandatory-2026-11-05/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Britain to make salary disclosure in job ads mandatory</span><span class='zh'>英国将强制要求招聘广告披露薪资</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Britain plans to make salary disclosure mandatory in job advertisements.</span><span class='zh'>英国计划强制招聘广告披露薪资信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Britain plans to make salary disclosure mandatory in job advertisements. Employers would have to include pay information when advertising open positions.</span><span class='zh'>英国计划强制招聘广告披露薪资信息。雇主发布职位空缺时将必须列出薪酬信息。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>577</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50389222' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>437</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50326099" data-id="50326099" data-tags="Security|Hardware" data-score="576" data-comments="177" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50326099" data-time="1793543683">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.reddit.com/r/technology/comments/1xl2pr3/lenovos_encrypted_fingerprint_usb_drive_is_just/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=reddit.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>reddit.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">43</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reddit.com/r/technology/comments/1xl2pr3/lenovos_encrypted_fingerprint_usb_drive_is_just/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lenovo&#x27;s &#x27;encrypted&#x27; fingerprint USB is just an unencrypted SD card in a case</span><span class='zh'>联想所谓的“加密”指纹 USB，其实只是装在外壳里的未加密 SD 卡</span></a><span class="dom">reddit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The title alleges that Lenovo’s fingerprint-protected USB device is not an encrypted drive, but an unencrypted SD card placed inside a casing.</span><span class='zh'>标题指称，联想这款受指纹保护的 USB 设备并非加密硬盘，而只是装在外壳中的未加密 SD 卡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The title alleges that Lenovo’s fingerprint-protected USB device is not an encrypted drive, but an unencrypted SD card placed inside a casing. It identifies a potential mismatch between the product’s security description and its underlying storage design.</span><span class='zh'>标题指称，联想这款受指纹保护的 USB 设备并非加密硬盘，而只是装在外壳中的未加密 SD 卡。这表明产品的安全描述可能与其底层存储设计不一致。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>576</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50326099' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>177</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50397429" data-id="50397429" data-tags="AI|Data" data-score="574" data-comments="146" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50397429" data-time="1793994531">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hightouch.com/blog/llm-judges-identity-resolution" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pwmfmi47/production/9a5d7191d614c824c822ec90672adaf424699d3d-2400x1256.png?w=1200' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='hightouch.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">44</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hightouch.com/blog/llm-judges-identity-resolution" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Jev that can ask Opus for help gives Opus accuracy at 4x lower cost</span><span class='zh'>能向 Opus 求助的 Jev，让 Opus 以低四倍成本达到同等准确率</span></a><span class="dom">hightouch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tests of Jev and GPT-6 Luna as judges for identity resolution found out-of-the-box accuracy ranging from 88.8% to 97.3%.</span><span class='zh'>对 Jev 和 GPT-6 Luna 作为身份解析评判器的测试显示，它们开箱即用的准确率为 88.8% 至 97.3%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tests of Jev and GPT-6 Luna as judges for identity resolution found out-of-the-box accuracy ranging from 88.8% to 97.3%. A hybrid that combines Jev with Claude Opus matched frontier-level accuracy at more than four times lower cost. Identity resolution maps data records to unique real-world people.</span><span class='zh'>对 Jev 和 GPT-6 Luna 作为身份解析评判器的测试显示，它们开箱即用的准确率为 88.8% 至 97.3%。将 Jev 与 Claude Opus 结合的混合方案达到了前沿模型水平的准确率，而成本降低了四倍以上。身份解析的任务是将数据记录映射到现实世界中唯一的个人。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>574</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50397429' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>146</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50383739" data-id="50383739" data-tags="Business" data-score="557" data-comments="212" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50383739" data-time="1793909009">
